# CERTIFIED COMPLAINER: Beats and Image Prompts (305)

Copy only the paragraph under each heading into your image generator. In Grok Imagine, select **Image** mode. Beats follow the natural pauses in the narration: quick cuts in the hook, never more than 5 seconds (2.5 words per second).

## Beat 1 (~2.4s)
*Script:* If complaining were an Olympic sport,

Generate a single still image, one frozen frame, not a video. A 3D-rendered illustration in Pixar/Disney feature-film style. Ogbonna standing on the top step of a sports podium in a packed stadium, a gold medal around his neck, frowning instead of smiling. Featuring Ogbonna, a tall, lean Igbo man in his early forties with rich brown skin, thick expressive eyebrows, deep frown lines, a short greying beard and a permanently unimpressed face, wearing a faded grey flat cap, a brown isiagu-patterned short-sleeve shirt and dark trousers, a carpenter's pencil tucked behind one ear. The setting is a packed sports stadium with a three-step winners' podium and colourful crowds in soft focus. The scene is lit by bright stadium floodlights with a warm golden glow and confetti in the air. The mood is comedic, grand. Framed as a medium, slight low angle shot with a mid-length lens and shallow depth of field, background softly blurred. Warm saturated colours of terracotta, ochre, danfo yellow, cobalt blue, purple and emerald.
